If the file has a source link to an existing Google Doc, the user MUST be prompted for confirmation before updating (updating overwrites the remote document). Use when the user says "push to google docs", "create google doc from markdown", "upload markdown to gdoc", "sync markdown to google docs", or wants to convert a .md file into a Google Doc.
